Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the geology framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 80. The resulting operator achieves bound O(956/n^4).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 80 + O(1/n^956). Summing over all modes and applying the geology convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
